# 2. TypeSpec:nexus-topology.tsp

This is youractual schema contract layer.

```
import "@typespec/json-schema";namespace Nexus.Topology {  // ----------------------------  // ENUMS  // ----------------------------  enum EntityKind {    system,    module,    service,    process,    runtime,    agent,    tool,    mcp,    schema,    ir,    memory_substrate,    capability_module,    harness_feature,    external_system,    ui_surface,    database,    projection,    snapshot,  }  enum IdentityStatus {    constitutive,    shared_substrate,    optional,    external_dependency,    experimental,    legacy,    unknown,  }  enum RelationshipType {    contains,    part_of,    depends_on,    uses,    invokes,    reads_from,    writes_to,    persists_to,    projects_to,    derived_from,    supersedes,    bridges_to,    optional_for,    required_for,    governed_by,    exposed_via,    mounted_into,  }  enum AssertionKind {    classification,    identity_boundary,    dependency_interpretation,    topology_claim,    responsibility_claim,    bridge_decision,    deprecation_claim,    uncertainty_claim,  }  enum EvidenceKind {    repo_scan,    import_graph,    runtime_observation,    config_observation,    document_passage,    transcript_quote,    change_log_event,    steward_annotation,    manual_note,  }  // ----------------------------  // CORE STRUCTURES  // ----------------------------  model TopologySnapshot {    snapshotId: string;    system: string;    capturedAt: utcDateTime;    scope: TopologyScope;    entityCount: int32;    edgeCount: int32;    assertionCount: int32;    confidenceScore: float32;    entities: TopologyEntity[];    edges: TopologyEdge[];    assertions: TopologyAssertion[];    evidence: TopologyEvidence[];    projections?: TopologyProjection[];    derivedFrom: string[];  }  model TopologyScope {    repos: string[];    branches?: string[];    workRequests?: string[];    harvestWindow?: HarvestWindow;  }  model HarvestWindow {    from: utcDateTime;    to: utcDateTime;  }  // ----------------------------  // ENTITY LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyEntity {    id: string;    name: string;    entityKind: EntityKind;    identityStatus: IdentityStatus;    topologyRole?: string;    description?: string;    systemAffinity: string[];    implementedBy?: string[];    consumedVia?: string[];    ownedContracts?: string[];    runtimePlacement?: RuntimePlacement;    tags?: string[];  }  model RuntimePlacement {    placementKind: string;    host?: string;    external?: boolean;  }  // ----------------------------  // EDGE LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyEdge {    id: string;    from: string;    to: string;    relationshipType: RelationshipType;    strength?: string;    cardinality?: string;    semanticMeaning?: string;    activationConditions?: string[];    evidenceRefs?: string[];  }  // ----------------------------  // ASSERTION LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyAssertion {    id: string;    subject: string;    predicate: string;    object: string;    assertionKind: AssertionKind;    scope: string;    confidence: float32;    status: string; // provisional | accepted | rejected    rationale?: string;    supportedBy?: string[];    assertedBy: string;    assertedAt: utcDateTime;  }  // ----------------------------  // EVIDENCE LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyEvidence {    id: string;    evidenceKind: EvidenceKind;    capturedAt: utcDateTime;    sourceRef: string;    summary: string;    supports: string[];    confidence: float32;  }  // ----------------------------  // PROJECTION LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyProjection {    id: string;    name: string;    projectionKind: string;    focusEntities?: string[];    focusAssertions?: string[];    derivedFromSnapshot: string;  }  // ----------------------------  // PROMOTION / GOVERNANCE  // ----------------------------  model TopologyPromotionDecision {    id: string;    candidateAssertion: string;    decision: string; // accepted | rejected | deferred    decidedBy: string;    decidedAt: utcDateTime;    resultingAssertion?: string;    notes?: string;  }}
```

import "@typespec/json-schema";namespace Nexus.Topology {  // ----------------------------  // ENUMS  // ----------------------------  enum EntityKind {    system,    module,    service,    process,    runtime,    agent,    tool,    mcp,    schema,    ir,    memory_substrate,    capability_module,    harness_feature,    external_system,    ui_surface,    database,    projection,    snapshot,  }  enum IdentityStatus {    constitutive,    shared_substrate,    optional,    external_dependency,    experimental,    legacy,    unknown,  }  enum RelationshipType {    contains,    part_of,    depends_on,    uses,    invokes,    reads_from,    writes_to,    persists_to,    projects_to,    derived_from,    supersedes,    bridges_to,    optional_for,    required_for,    governed_by,    exposed_via,    mounted_into,  }  enum AssertionKind {    classification,    identity_boundary,    dependency_interpretation,    topology_claim,    responsibility_claim,    bridge_decision,    deprecation_claim,    uncertainty_claim,  }  enum EvidenceKind {    repo_scan,    import_graph,    runtime_observation,    config_observation,    document_passage,    transcript_quote,    change_log_event,    steward_annotation,    manual_note,  }  // ----------------------------  // CORE STRUCTURES  // ----------------------------  model TopologySnapshot {    snapshotId: string;    system: string;    capturedAt: utcDateTime;    scope: TopologyScope;    entityCount: int32;    edgeCount: int32;    assertionCount: int32;    confidenceScore: float32;    entities: TopologyEntity[];    edges: TopologyEdge[];    assertions: TopologyAssertion[];    evidence: TopologyEvidence[];    projections?: TopologyProjection[];    derivedFrom: string[];  }  model TopologyScope {    repos: string[];    branches?: string[];    workRequests?: string[];    harvestWindow?: HarvestWindow;  }  model HarvestWindow {    from: utcDateTime;    to: utcDateTime;  }  // ----------------------------  // ENTITY LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyEntity {    id: string;    name: string;    entityKind: EntityKind;    identityStatus: IdentityStatus;    topologyRole?: string;    description?: string;    systemAffinity: string[];    implementedBy?: string[];    consumedVia?: string[];    ownedContracts?: string[];    runtimePlacement?: RuntimePlacement;    tags?: string[];  }  model RuntimePlacement {    placementKind: string;    host?: string;    external?: boolean;  }  // ----------------------------  // EDGE LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyEdge {    id: string;    from: string;    to: string;    relationshipType: RelationshipType;    strength?: string;    cardinality?: string;    semanticMeaning?: string;    activationConditions?: string[];    evidenceRefs?: string[];  }  // ----------------------------  // ASSERTION LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyAssertion {    id: string;    subject: string;    predicate: string;    object: string;    assertionKind: AssertionKind;    scope: string;    confidence: float32;    status: string; // provisional | accepted | rejected    rationale?: string;    supportedBy?: string[];    assertedBy: string;    assertedAt: utcDateTime;  }  // ----------------------------  // EVIDENCE LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyEvidence {    id: string;    evidenceKind: EvidenceKind;    capturedAt: utcDateTime;    sourceRef: string;    summary: string;    supports: string[];    confidence: float32;  }  // ----------------------------  // PROJECTION LAYER  // ----------------------------  model TopologyProjection {    id: string;    name: string;    projectionKind: string;    focusEntities?: string[];    focusAssertions?: string[];    derivedFromSnapshot: string;  }  // ----------------------------  // PROMOTION / GOVERNANCE  // ----------------------------  model TopologyPromotionDecision {    id: string;    candidateAssertion: string;    decision: string; // accepted | rejected | deferred    decidedBy: string;    decidedAt: utcDateTime;    resultingAssertion?: string;    notes?: string;  }}

# 3. JSON-LD sample instance (LOSM example)

This shows how the schema actuallybehavesas a graph.

```
{  "@context": "nexus-topology.context.jsonld",  "@type": "TopologyEntity",  "entityId": "entity:losm",  "name": "LOSM",  "entityKind": "capability_module",  "identityStatus": "optional",  "topologyRole": "semantic_execution_engine",  "systemAffinity": ["Nexus", "Assistance"],  "description": "Optional semantic execution engine used for graph-based reasoning and process orchestration.",  "implementedBy": [    "repo:losm-kernel",    "repo:losm-shell",    "repo:losm-host"  ],  "runtimePlacement": {    "placementKind": "external_process",    "external": true  }}
```

```
{  "@context": "nexus-topology.context.jsonld",  "@type": "TopologyEntity",  "entityId": "entity:losm",  "name": "LOSM",  "entityKind": "capability_module",  "identityStatus": "optional",  "topologyRole": "semantic_execution_engine",  "systemAffinity": ["Nexus", "Assistance"],  "description": "Optional semantic execution engine used for graph-based reasoning and process orchestration.",  "implementedBy": [    "repo:losm-kernel",    "repo:losm-shell",    "repo:losm-host"  ],  "runtimePlacement": {    "placementKind": "external_process",    "external": true  }}
```

# 4. What this buys you immediately

This schema is not just representation — it gives youthree hard capabilities:

---

## 1. Self-inventory

Nexus can answer:

- what exists
what exists

- where it lives
where it lives

- what kind of thing it is
what kind of thing it is

---

## 2. Self-interpretation

Because of:

- identityStatus
identityStatus

- TopologyAssertion
TopologyAssertion

- TopologyRole
TopologyRole

Nexus can say:

- “LOSM is optional”
“LOSM is optional”

- “PEB is constitutive”
“PEB is constitutive”

- “OpenCode is external harness state”
“OpenCode is external harness state”

---

## 3. Self-topology explanation

Because of edges + assertions:

- “what uses what”
“what uses what”

- “why it exists”
“why it exists”

- “what would break if removed”
“what would break if removed”

---

# 5. The key design win (this is the important part)

This schema cleanly separates:

## Structure (truth)

- entities
entities

- edges
edges

## Meaning (interpretation)

- assertions
assertions

- identityStatus
identityStatus

- topologyRole
topologyRole

## Evidence (epistemology)

- why we believe anything at all
why we believe anything at all

That separation is what makes:

> “Nexus can tell us what it is and how it thinks about itself”

“Nexus can tell us what it is and how it thinks about itself”

actually work instead of becoming a static diagram.

---

If you want the next step, the natural continuation is:

a query layer for this model(e.g. “show all optional capability modules and what they depend on” or “explain Nexus core identity boundary”), which is where it starts becoming a real introspection engine instead of just a schema.
